# ESS-1000 — Essential Completeness Lattice

Status: **design complete — 0/1000 implementation-signed**

ESS-1000 defines **1000 non-compensable essentials** for the complete Skeleton AI platform, organized as **100 strata × 10 stages**.

This is intentionally different from Frontier-96, CS-300, Learning-400/Adversarial-400 and PSI-1000. Those ladders increase capability, knowledge or improvement depth. **ESS-1000 prevents sophistication from hiding missing basics.**

A faster model does not compensate for broken authorization. A better benchmark does not compensate for an untested restore path. A clever mirror room does not compensate for missing idempotency. More agents do not compensate for ambiguous authority. More memory does not compensate for deletion failure.

## Essential law

Every ESS level is a floor, not a stretch goal.

```text
essential missing
  -> platform gap remains open
  -> related advanced capability cannot erase the gap
  -> PSI mirror rooms prioritize closure in that affected domain
  -> exact implementation + tests + failure semantics + recovery + independent evidence
  -> essential may be signed
```

Documentation alone is not implementation. A disabled optional capability still needs a safe disabled-state control path. "Not applicable" cannot be used to remove platform-level obligations.

## Ten macro-groups

- **ESS-M01 — Purpose & Architecture:** ESS1000-0001..ESS1000-0100
- **ESS-M02 — State & Persistence:** ESS1000-0101..ESS1000-0200
- **ESS-M03 — Interfaces & Security:** ESS1000-0201..ESS1000-0300
- **ESS-M04 — Isolation & Change Control:** ESS1000-0301..ESS1000-0400
- **ESS-M05 — Delivery & Test Discipline:** ESS1000-0401..ESS1000-0500
- **ESS-M06 — Reliability & Recovery:** ESS1000-0501..ESS1000-0600
- **ESS-M07 — Operability & Efficiency:** ESS1000-0601..ESS1000-0700
- **ESS-M08 — Infrastructure & Data:** ESS1000-0701..ESS1000-0800
- **ESS-M09 — AI Runtime & Assurance:** ESS1000-0801..ESS1000-0900
- **ESS-M10 — Human/Product/Governance Finality:** ESS1000-0901..ESS1000-1000

## 100 strata × 10 stages

### ESS-S001 — Mission & Product Intent

Scope: mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes.

- **ESS1000-0001 — Mission & Product Intent — Essential Invariant & Minimum.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, define the irreducible minimum, protected invariant, ownership, scope, forbidden shortcuts and the exact condition that would constitute a missing essential. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0002 — Mission & Product Intent — Canonical Contract & Identity.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, encode typed identities, schemas, interfaces, lifecycle states, compatibility rules and authoritative sources so the essential is machine-addressable. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0003 — Mission & Product Intent — Baseline Implementation.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, implement the minimum reliable mechanism without optional sophistication; prefer boring deterministic behavior over cleverness. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0004 — Mission & Product Intent — Failure & Negative Semantics.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, define malformed, unavailable, partial, stale, duplicated, unauthorized, overloaded and corrupted behavior and require explicit fail-closed or degraded outcomes. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0005 — Mission & Product Intent — Verification & Test Floor.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, establish deterministic positive, negative, boundary, replay and regression tests that would fail if the essential disappears or becomes semantically weaker. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0006 — Mission & Product Intent — Observability & Diagnosis.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, make essential state, failure, latency, resource behavior and evidence diagnosable with bounded logs, metrics, traces or equivalent receipts. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0007 — Mission & Product Intent — Security, Privacy & Isolation Gate.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, prove the essential does not weaken authority, secrets, tenant/project isolation, privacy, data lifecycle or supply-chain boundaries. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0008 — Mission & Product Intent — Recovery, Compatibility & Rollback.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, prove upgrade, downgrade where supported, migration, restore, rollback, cancellation and interrupted-operation behavior. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0009 — Mission & Product Intent — Independent Exact-Head Evidence.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, produce independently reproducible exact-head evidence bound to implementation identity, tests, configuration and relevant artifacts. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.
- **ESS1000-0010 — Mission & Product Intent — Non-Compensable Essential Closure.** For mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes, sign the essential complete only when all prior stages are current; advanced capability, benchmark gain or optional feature depth cannot compensate for a failure here. This is a minimum completeness requirement, not an optimization target. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must prove the minimum remains present and correctly wired for mission, user value, boundaries, non-goals, stakeholders and measurable outcomes; demonstrate failure behavior and regression detection, preserve all non-compensable invariants, and bind the result to the current canonical masterplan and implementation identity.

## Relationship to PSI-1000

Every `ESS1000-nnnn` is paired to `PSI1000-nnnn`.

When an essential is unresolved in an affected domain, the idle scheduler must rank essential closure or regression repair above optional cosmetic/polish work in that domain, absent a more severe non-compensable incident elsewhere.

PSI evidence may be reused as factual evidence if it is exact, current and independently reproducible, but PSI completion does **not** automatically sign ESS completion.

## Finality

`ESS1000-1000` is the whole-system essential finality gate. It cannot self-attest.

ESS-1000 remains incomplete until all **1000/1000** levels are simultaneously signed complete on current evidence. Initial implementation state is deliberately **0/1000**.
